Ring count is correct (10 synthetic verticals at 245.3 px pitch = 1226 mm, ring_count_error 0, det_fallback_ratio 1.0, det_real_detection_ratio 0 which is expected under uniform_k_snap on continuous). Detection: oblique Hough only fires around the K row (green ~y1440, red ~y1610, one blue at ~y780) and only for x < 1600; the other longitudinal seams at y~780, ~2280 and ~2950 are clearly visible in the depth map (bolt rows + dotted seam pixels in the binary image) but yield no lines because the seam pixels are dotted and fall below the oblique accumulator / are broken by gaps. The SC overlay confirms this: label boundaries at ~98, ~795, ~2260, ~2950 are red (unsupported) and most joint lines near K are orange (offset from label edges), giving correspondence_f1@15 = 0.244. SAM: sam_fill_rate 0.571, ring completeness 0.89, Ring 0 (leftmost) is narrow/partial and ring template edges drift relative to the 1226 mm measured pitch when segment_width is 1200 mm.
- Rule: experiences §7 tuning order + §12: recentre residual >> 10 cm with stage1_unlocked is an ontology trigger to touch unfolding and run stages 1-6 (f_curve_overfit / end-slice ellipse contamination -> off-centre curve at one end). Then ontology d6/d7 -> f_missing_lines: seams visible in the depth map but too few oblique lines and horizontal fallback / propagation only -> r_relax_hough (lower oblique threshold, raise maxLineGap, lower binary threshold) after ruling out gross depth holes (NaN is family-normal, joints are visible). Keep ring count consistent (experiences §5: line count == ring count matters more than more lines) so vertical Hough stays in the synthetic regime. SAM geometry only as a light, measured correction (§6 graded regime): match segment_width to the observed ring pitch. Do not touch denoising geometry (§4: keep r-band at tunnel priors) or orientation (§10).
- Failure mode: stage_1_centreline.f_curve_overfit / end-slice contamination (residual 20.8 cm, one-end void) -> stage_4_detection.f_missing_lines (only K-row obliques, seams at other label boundaries undetected -> low correspondence F1) -> stage_5_segmentation.f_crop_offset / f_over_under_coverage (template pitch 1200 vs measured 1226 mm, fill rate 0.57).
- Rationale: Round 1 of 3: fix upstream first. (1) Unfolding: tighten ransac_threshold 1.0 -> 0.85 so the deviating end-slice ellipse centre is treated as an outlier of the degree-2 curve instead of bending it, and re-roll random_seed (3) so the stage-1 fit is re-drawn under the tightened threshold; orientation is canonical/pinned so a re-roll cannot flip handedness. Keep polynomial_degree 2 (10 slice centres, cubic would overfit) and leave slice_spacing_factor alone so ring_count stays 10. (2) Detecting: binary_threshold 127 -> 115 keeps the fainter dotted seam pixels at y~780/2280/2950, hough_threshold_oblique 30 -> 24 and maxLineGap_oblique 30 -> 45 let those dotted seams accumulate into oblique segments, hough_threshold_horizontal 30 -> 26 gives the fallback a slightly better chance on the flattest seams; hough_threshold_vertical set high (2200) to remain in the synthetic 10-vertical regime so ring_count_error stays 0; pattern_tolerance 10 and uniform_k_snap true keep the K-row gate strict so the extra lines cannot move the propagated K row. Expected effect: more label boundaries explained by lines -> correspondence_f1@15 up. (3) SAM: segment_width 1200 -> 1225 mm matches the measured 245.3 px ring pitch so per-ring templates stop drifting across the 10 rings, which should lift sam_fill_rate without changing K_height / angle (left at priors). Denoising and enhancing untouched (r-band and theta gate at T3 priors; the confirmed causal depth thresholds are not in bounds). full=true because unfolding changes.

Proxy reading (§9): a proxy gain carried by a coherence feature (F1) while fill rate drops is only half a win; keep the detection direction that paid off (ontology d7 / f_missing_lines -> r_relax_hough) and recover coverage via stage-3 densification (ontology f_depth_holes -> r_increase_window_or_upsample: the only in-bounds stage-3 knob is curvature_threshold, raising it lets more near-flat lining points be surface-densified so small bolt-hole halos close). ## 3-4 — round 3

- Observation: Round 2 (stage 1 frozen on the anchor map, residual back to 20.8) scored 0.530: correspondence_f1@15 0.269 (above anchor 0.244, below round-1's 0.286 which was measured on a different, re-rolled map), sam_fill_rate 0.554 (below anchor 0.571 on the same map), depth_nan_ratio 0.190 (best so far). Like-for-like against the anchor, the detection relaxation is worth about +0.025 F1, while curvature_threshold 0.0008 produced exactly the suspect pattern from the experiences: NaN down but fill rate down, i.e. the newly densified pixels sit in bolt-hole halos / seam grooves that the SAM block masks do not cover. Round-2 detected_lines shows the more aggressive thresholds (108/22/55) only added short red/green fragments in the noisy left columns (x < 350 at y~150, ~930, ~1290, ~2150, ~2200, ~2320, ~2440) and still no oblique on the y~780 / y~2280 seams, so the Hough relaxation is saturated and further steps only cost precision. The dominant remaining coverage defect is visible in depth_map_viridis: every bolt pocket is a yellow (deep) rim around a white hole, the K-row joint groove at y~1480 is a white band, and the bottom-right patch is a fractal white region, all consistent with points whose radius exceeds the T3 r_outer = 3.0 m gate being discarded (f_over_gate on recessed lining), not with centreline error. Ring count 10 / error 0, K row propagated at y~1515, labels in correct ring order (A2|A1|B1|K|B2|A3|A2), orientation pinned.
- Rule: experiences §9: a NaN-driven proxy move with falling fill rate is cosmetic -> revert curvature_threshold to the prior. Ontology d3/f_over_gate -> r_radial_gate_widen: when holes coincide with recessed lining features (bolt pockets, joint groove) widen [r_inner, r_outer] toward the prior radius_range, here only r_outer, staying inside the given bound. §4: geometry knobs have small isolated effects, so keep the step small and leave r_inner / theta gates at T3 priors (the top fixture band and floor edge are physical, and lowering r_inner would leak brackets = f_under_gate). §10: do not re-roll stage 1 (residual is structural and recentred; round 1 proved reseeding does nothing) -> full=false. §5: keep verticals synthetic so ring_count_error stays 0. Consolidate the detection settings that were validated (round-1 values) rather than the saturated round-2 ones.
- Failure mode: stage_2_denoise.f_over_gate on recessed lining (bolt pockets, K-row groove, bottom-right patch) -> stage_3_upsample.f_depth_holes -> stage_5_segmentation.f_over_under_coverage (fill rate 0.55-0.57); secondary stage_4_detection.f_missing_lines on the ~780/~2280 seams, partially remedied and now saturated.
- Rationale: Final round, stage 1 frozen (full=false). (1) Denoising: mask_r_high 3.0 -> 3.06 m to retain the deeper bolt-pocket and joint-groove points that currently become NaN halos; these pixels lie inside the SAM block templates, so recovering them should raise sam_fill_rate and denoise_retained_ratio and lower depth_nan_ratio together (the healthy, non-cosmetic pattern), and the extra depth contrast at groove bottoms feeds the joint-outlier enhancement rather than washing it out. mask_r_low and theta gates untouched. (2) Enhancing: curvature_threshold back to the 0.0005 prior (round-2 evidence: cosmetic). (3) Detecting: consolidate the round-1 settings that produced the best F1 (binary_threshold 115, hough_threshold_oblique 24, maxLineGap_oblique 45, hough_threshold_horizontal 26) instead of the saturated round-2 values that only added left-edge fragments; hough_threshold_vertical 2200 keeps the synthetic 10 verticals (ring_count_error 0); pattern_tolerance 10 and uniform_k_snap true keep the K-row gate strict. (4) SAM: segment_width 1200 (prior), K_height and angle at priors since the detected K band (~170 px) and per-ring oblique slope (~5-6 deg) match 823.8 mm / 6.12 deg. Expected: fill rate back above the anchor with F1 retained near the round-1 level, i.e. the first round where both coherence features move up on the same map.

- Rule: sam4tun_ontology d7 (m_line_yield low AND horizontal fallback triggered -> f_missing_lines) with upstream check d6 (m_joint_contrast low -> f_joint_oversmoothed) via inter_stage_dependency stage_3_upsample.f_joint_oversmoothed -> stage_4_detection.f_missing_lines. Remediations r_relax_hough (lower oblique threshold / raise maxLineGap) and the stage-3 joint-enhancement side (inter_radius, since depth_threshold_low/high are not in the bounded set). experiences.md section 7: stages 1-3 validated as healthy this round, so touching detection is allowed; section 5: keep vertical count == ring count, so hough_threshold_vertical is left untouched.

- Rationale: Coordinated stage-3 + stage-4 move aimed directly at correspondence_f1@15. (1) enhancing.inter_radius 0.06 -> 0.075 (+15 px -> ~15 px interpolation reach) bridges dashed joint points into continuous boundary traces before projection, raising joint contrast in the outlier map. (2) detecting.binary_threshold 127 -> 118 admits slightly fainter joint pixels after dilation. (3) detecting.hough_threshold_oblique -> 44 and detecting.maxLineGap_oblique -> 60 let the [5,10]/[-10,-5] deg accumulator accept the longer but still gapped oblique joints at the A/B boundaries (K +/- 648 px) and in the low-density columns; the angle filter and the K/AB distance-pattern gate limit spurious acceptances. (4) detecting.hough_threshold_horizontal -> 65 suppresses the unexplained horizontal fallback lines (orange in the overlay) that dilute correspondence precision; horizontal is fallback-only and did not rescue the single 'assume' prompt anyway. Vertical threshold untouched (10 lines == 10 rings). Denoising left at tunnel priors (retention healthy; experiences section 4). SAM y_bounds not changed: sam_fill_rate 0.73 losses coincide with the physical void bands at y~250 and y~2500, not with a graded per-class weakness, so SAM geometry is not the identified failure this round. Unfolding omitted (stage1_unlocked false, residual 1.4 cm). (2) detecting.hough_threshold_horizontal -> 90 (bound max) to suppress the persistent unexplained horizontal fallback lines that lower correspondence precision and never contributed a correct prompt. (3) enhancing.inter_radius -> 0.05 (slightly below the 0.06 default; reverting the 0.075 increase that over-interpolated the dense region) to reduce interpolated speckle in the outlier map at source while leaving real joint traces, which are already continuous in those columns. (4) binary_threshold dropped from the overlay (reverts to anchor 127) - the outlier map is near-binary, so the round-1 change was inert. Vertical threshold unchanged (10 == 10). The resulting prompt centres are staggered consistently for rings 0-3 (y 1120/1555/1195/1630, alternating +-435 px, two of them pattern-assumed) but drift for rings 4-9 (1400, 1630, 1920, 1890, 2060, 2270), i.e. the midpoints computed in the dense region are corrupted. The SC overlay confirms this: most label boundaries are red (unsupported) and most joint lines are orange (not explained), correspondence_f1@15 = 0.20 while sam_fill_rate is a moderate 0.73.
- Rule: d9 (spurious lines -> f_spurious_lines) with d7 partially firing (horizontal fallback used on the sparse half); inter-stage chain f_spurious_lines/f_pattern_mismatch -> stage_5 f_crop_offset. Remediation r_tighten_hough (raise oblique threshold, shorten maxLineGap so lines stay ring-local, raise horizontal threshold to suppress bolt-hole-row fallbacks, raise binary threshold slightly to drop dilated speckle) paired with a mild inter_radius increase so genuine joint segments in the sparse half gain enough interpolated support to survive the stricter accumulator. Experiences section 7: stages 1-3 validated, so detection is the correct stage to touch this round; do not touch vertical threshold (line count already equals ring count) or stage-1 keys (locked).
- Failure mode: stage_4_detection.f_spurious_lines (multi-ring oblique lines and bolt-hole-row horizontals in the dense right half) producing drifting prompt centres for rings 4-9 -> stage_5_segmentation.f_crop_offset -> label boundaries misaligned with joint lines (low correspondence F1).
- Rationale: Correspondence F1 is the feature dragging the proxy into the refine band while evidence features (NaN, retention, ring count) are already healthy, so the round-1 budget goes to prompt-centre quality. Raising hough_threshold_oblique 50->65 and cutting maxLineGap_oblique 40->25 demands more collinear support and prevents bridging across ring joints and bolt-hole gaps, so the long multi-ring oblique lines are rejected while ring-local joints (~240 px, 7.5 deg taper) remain detectable. Raising hough_threshold_horizontal 50->65 suppresses the horizontal fallbacks that lock onto bolt-hole rows and void edges. binary_threshold 127->135 removes weak dilated speckle from the noise cloud around x~1600, y~1450 before Hough. inter_radius 0.06->0.07 mildly densifies interpolated joint points so the sparse left half still yields real detections under the stricter thresholds; it is kept small to avoid re-amplifying noise on the right half. Vertical threshold, denoising geometry (kept at priors per experiences section 4) and unfolding (stage1_unlocked=false) are left untouched. Expected effect: fewer spurious oblique/horizontal lines, prompt centres for rings 4-9 return to the +-435 px stagger seen in rings 0-3, label boundaries align with joint lines, correspondence_f1@15 and sam_fill_rate rise. Risk: det_real_detection_ratio may dip slightly if more rings fall to pattern-assumed centres, but the assumed centres on rings 0-1 were already more consistent than the corrupted midpoints.

## 1-4 — round 2

- Observation: Round 1 (relax oblique Hough + lower binarisation + inter_radius 0.08) moved the proxy the wrong way: 0.646 -> 0.627, correspondence_f1@15 0.395 -> 0.360, sam_fill_rate flat (0.699 -> 0.698), ring_count_error still 0, NaN and residual unchanged. The updated detected_lines.png shows the extra oblique segments are mostly spurious: a red segment on the bolt row at y~960 px (x 1750-1900), a green segment on the bottom margin at y~2640 px, and the left-edge red at x<110 px, while real joints still appear as dotted traces. initial_prompt_points.png now has only 4 real intersections (2 negative-slope at y~1185, 2 positive-slope at y~1615), 4 'assume' and 2 midpoints, one of which sits off-row at y~1560 px. So relaxing detection traded a few real joints for noise lines and shifted prompts off the K-row lattice, which is exactly what depressed the correspondence proxy. Upstream health is unchanged (residual 1.9 cm, corr -0.995, NaN 0.15, retention 0.72), so stages 1-3 remain non-suspects.
- Rule: d9 (spurious lines / off-lattice prompts after relaxation) -> f_spurious_lines; remediation r_tighten_hough (raise oblique threshold, shorten maxLineGap) and restore the default binarisation. Because f_joint_oversmoothed (d6) is still the plausible root cause and depth_threshold_low/high are not exposed, use the remaining stage-3 lever in the opposite sense to round 1: lower enhancing.curvature_threshold so high-curvature joint neighbourhoods are not treated as flat surface and smoothed by enhance_segment_surface, preserving joint contrast in the outlier map. Tuning-order rule: do not touch denoise geometry (retention healthy, transfer prior) or the vertical accumulator (10/10 rings).
- Failure mode: stage_4_detection.f_spurious_lines (induced by round-1 relaxation) on top of residual stage_3_upsample.f_joint_oversmoothed
- Rationale: Round 1 is a clean negative result: more oblique lines were not better, consistent with experiences sec.5 (consistency beats count). Round 2 therefore explicitly reverts the relaxation (inter_radius back to 0.06, binary_threshold back to 127, hough_threshold_horizontal back to 50) and moves the oblique detector to the conservative side: hough_threshold_oblique 60 and maxLineGap_oblique 30 so only well-supported +-5-10 deg traces survive, suppressing the bolt-row and margin false positives that produced off-lattice midpoints. Fewer but truer intersections should keep every prompt on the two K-row loci (y~1185 / y~1615 px) and let the 'assume' fill-in reproduce the staggered pattern faithfully. In parallel, curvature_threshold is lowered from the 0.0005 default to 0.0003 so fewer near-joint points are densified as surface, sharpening joint traces without changing the depth map coverage (NaN should stay ~0.15). Expected GT-free effect: correspondence_f1@15 back above the anchor 0.395, det_fallback_ratio <= 0.3 with no off-row midpoints, ring_count_error 0, sam_fill_rate >= 0.70. If f1 does not recover, round 3 should treat detection as saturated and hold the anchor detection while probing the SAM row geometry.

## 1-1 — round 2

- Observation: Round 1 (relaxed Hough/binarisation + slightly stronger joint enhancement) did NOT change the detection regime: det_fallback_ratio stayed 0.3, det_n_points stayed 10, depth_outlier_ratio moved only 0.0032 -> 0.0034, and the outlier map is visually identical (still dense only for x > ~1200, empty on the left half). What did change was the y-placement of three prompts (x=1331: 1640 -> 1939; x=1815: 2270 -> 2288; x=2058 midpoint -> negative_slope), and correspondence_f1@15 fell 0.294 -> 0.200 while sam_fill_rate (0.723 -> 0.724) and ring_count_error (0) were flat, so proxy_scaled fell 0.607 -> 0.554. Conclusion: the missing left-half joint evidence is not a Hough/binarisation sensitivity problem; the joint points are absent before detection. Inspecting the applied anchor parameters shows the denoising radial gate is mask_r = [2.33, 2.78] m for a 5.5 m (r = 2.75 m) tunnel with a 2.8 cm recentre residual. Lining points sit at r ~ 2.75 +/- 0.03 and segment joints are recessed (larger r), so the outer gate at 2.78 clips exactly the recessed joint points and outer lining on the far (sparse, left-half) side of the map, which is where the outlier map goes empty. The tunnel prior radius_range outer is 2.8, above the anchor's 2.78.
- Rule: Ontology inter_stage_dependency stage_2_denoise.f_over_gate -> stage_3_upsample.f_depth_holes / low m_joint_contrast -> stage_4_detection.f_missing_lines; remediation r_radial_gate_widen ('widen [r_inner, r_outer] toward prior radius_range', precondition: confirm prior radius_range = 2.7-2.8 for T1). Also r_relax_hough precondition ('first rule out upstream f_joint_oversmoothed / f_depth_holes') was violated in round 1, which is consistent with its null effect. experiences.md §7 tuning order: band selection (stage 2) must be validated before detection; §10 anti-pattern 'tuning Hough while an upstream coverage defect is active'.
- Failure mode: f_over_gate (stage 2, outer radial gate too tight at 2.78 m relative to r=2.75 m lining + 2.8 cm residual + recessed joints) -> f_joint_oversmoothed/f_missing_lines downstream: recessed joint points are discarded before enhancement, so the outlier map has no joint evidence on the far side and 30% of prompts are assumed.
- Rationale: Single-stage, attributable move: raise denoising.mask_r_high 2.78 -> 2.86 (inside [2.7, 2.9]; covers r_lining 2.75 + 0.028 residual + a few cm of joint recess, still below the 2.9 bound to limit far-wall noise). Points beyond the lining surface are mostly multipath/noise, so the risk of background leakage is low and would show up as a rise in depth_outlier_ratio and det_n_points, which I will watch. mask_r_low is left at 2.33 (experiences §4: inner gate is load-bearing for transfer; the inner clutter is not the failure here). Enhancing and detecting overlays from round 1 are dropped so they revert to anchor values: the round-1 detection relaxation lowered F1 by shifting prompt y-positions without adding real detections, and reverting isolates the effect of the gate change. SAM unchanged (fill_rate/ring_completeness are downstream symptoms). Expected GT-free signal: denoise_retained_ratio up from 0.753, depth_nan_ratio down from 0.107, outlier map gains joint pixels on the left half, det_fallback_ratio 0.3 -> lower, correspondence_f1@15 back above 0.294, ring_count_error stays 0. If retained ratio rises but F1 does not, round 3 will combine the wider gate with detection/enhancement tuning now that the upstream precondition is met.
- Overlay: `{"denoising":{"mask_r_high":2.86}}`

## 3-6 — round 1

- Observation: Stages 1-3 are healthy: recentre residual 3.2 cm (<10 cm gate), orient_axis_corr -0.9998 with invariant ok, depth NaN 10.5% (below the ~19-22% family-normal), denoise retention 0.69 with spatially uniform discards, ring_count 10 == vertical line count. The proxy is held in the refine band (0.681) mainly by correspondence_f1@15 = 0.467 and sam_fill_rate = 0.649. In the SC overlay only the upper K-row boundary (~y=1475) is supported (green); the lower K boundary (~y=1675) sits ~40-50 px below the red/orange oblique joint lines detected at y~1620-1640, and the A/B block boundaries at y~800 and y~2350 are red even though the depth map shows clear horizontal joint rows (double bolt-pocket rows + seam) exactly there; only a handful of short blue horizontal segments were found on them. Oblique lines are fragmented (short pieces per column), consistent with the binarised joint map being sparse.
- Rule: experiences.md §7 tuning order: orientation/centreline/band all validate, so detection (stage 4) and SAM geometry (stage 5) are the identifiable knobs. Ontology d7 (m_line_yield low, horizontal fallback) -> f_missing_lines -> r_relax_hough (lower oblique/horizontal Hough threshold, raise maxLineGap) after ruling out f_joint_oversmoothed / f_depth_holes (both ruled out here). Ontology r_fix_template: set K_height / taper angle to the T3 priors (823.8 mm, 6.12 deg) when label block boundaries disagree with detected joint lines. Continuous family stays on uniform_k_snap (regime, not quality).
- Failure mode: stage_4_detection.f_missing_lines (fragmented oblique + horizontal joint lines, leaving most A/B label boundaries unsupported) compounded by a mild stage_5_segmentation.f_template_mismatch (K block labelled taller than the detected K joint pair).
- Rationale: Lower binary_threshold (127 -> 110) and oblique/horizontal Hough thresholds (30 -> 24) with a larger oblique maxLineGap (30 -> 42) to recover continuous joint lines across the bolt-pocket rows at y~800 and y~2350 and to lengthen the K-row oblique segments, which should raise recall of label boundaries within 15 px; pattern_tolerance 12 px keeps the K/AB distance check permissive enough for the added lines without accepting spurious intersections. On the SAM side pin K_height and taper angle to the T3 priors (823.8 mm, 6.12 deg) so the K block template height matches the detected joint pair (~170 px = 850 mm) and the lower K boundary moves up to the detected line; segment_width stays at the 1200 mm prior. Unfolding is untouched (stage1 locked); denoising is left at the anchor band because retention and NaN are healthy.

So the remaining detection defect is spurious short oblique fragments in the dense band pulling intersections off the true seam rows, not a shortage of lines; a further relaxation (round 1) would make that worse, which is exactly what was measured.
- Rule: Ontology diagnostic d9 / failure f_spurious_lines ('extra intersections; false prompt centres') -> remediation r_tighten_hough ('raise oblique Hough threshold / minLineLength; check upstream f_under_gate'). Precondition satisfied: round 2 showed the stage-2 gate is now healthy (retention up, NaN flat, no rise in depth_outlier_ratio), so per experiences.md §7 (band selection validated before detection) and §5 ('more detected lines is not better; consistency with ring count is what matters') detection may now be tuned. hough_threshold_vertical is left alone because vertical count already equals ring count (10/10) and §5 warns that changing it risks the 11-for-10 truncation failure.
- Failure mode: f_spurious_lines (stage 4): binarisation at 127 plus a permissive oblique HoughLinesP (threshold 50, maxLineGap 40) accepts short speckle-bridged oblique fragments in the dense up-sampled band; their intersections with the vertical ring lines produce prompt centres off the K/AB rows and propagate as midpoint prompts to neighbouring rings (stage 5 f_crop_offset). Upstream f_over_gate has been resolved in round 2 and is kept resolved here.
- Rationale: Coordinated two-stage overlay that keeps the proven round-2 gate and tightens detection in the opposite direction to round 1. (1) denoising.mask_r_high stays 2.86 (round-2 value; the only move so far with a positive attributable effect). (2) detecting.binary_threshold 127 -> 135 so faint speckle in the dense band drops out of the binarised outlier map while the strong joint pixels (which survive at 127 with margin) are retained; not pushed to the 160 bound because the left half is already faint. (3) detecting.hough_threshold_oblique 50 -> 60 so an oblique line needs more supporting pixels (a real seam spanning a segment width ~240 px easily clears this; a 60-100 px speckle chain does not). (4) detecting.maxLineGap_oblique 40 -> 30 so unrelated fragments are no longer bridged into one long false seam. Enhancing is left at anchor values: the round-1 enhancing changes are confounded with the detection relaxation and cannot be credited, and re-adding them would re-introduce the suspect. hough_threshold_horizontal and hough_threshold_vertical unchanged (horizontal is fallback-only; vertical count is already 10/10). SAM y_bounds unchanged: fill_rate/ring_completeness are downstream of prompt placement. All values inside bounds (binary [100,160], oblique [40,90], maxLineGap [20,80], mask_r_high [2.7,2.9]); stage1_unlocked is false so no unfolding keys. Expected GT-free signal: fewer orange (unexplained) oblique lines on the overlay, prompt y-positions clustering onto the K/AB rows, correspondence_f1@15 >= 0.364, det_n_points stays 10, ring_count_error 0, sam_fill_rate not falling. Risk: if det_fallback_ratio rises above 0.3 (real oblique detections lost), the tightening was too strong and the round-2 overlay (mask_r_high 2.86 only) remains the fallback selection.

Expected: more real prompt points (det_real_detection_ratio up), more label boundaries supported by lines (F1 up), ring count unchanged, NaN unchanged.

Relaxing Hough added more short, unexplained oblique/horizontal fragments (precision loss) without giving SAM any new boundary to agree with.

On SAM, keep segment_width 1200 and angle 6.12 (vertical spacing ~240 px = 1200 mm matches, taper only affects x-boundaries) but reduce K_height 823.8 -> 760 mm (152 px): the template becomes ~13 px shorter on each side than the detected pair, so the B2 template starts inside the pocket band and its prompts compete for it, pulling the K/B2 label boundary up from ~1675 toward the detected ~1630 line and shifting the B2/A3 boundary from ~2350 toward the detected ~2300 row. Stop at 720 rather than the 700 floor so the upper K boundary (which is already supported at ~1475 by the green family) is not dragged below its joint: a 4 px per-side shrink keeps it inside the 15 px tolerance even if SAM follows the template edge exactly. segment_width stays 1200 (vertical ring spacing ~240 px matches) and angle stays at the T3 taper prior 6.12 because taper only affects x-boundaries and there is no ring-boundary miss in the overlay. Unfolding is untouched (stage 1 locked, residual 3.2 cm); denoising and enhancing stay at anchor because retention, NaN and joint contrast are all healthy and none of the evidence features moved in rounds 1-2. Expected outcome: F1@15 up, sam_fill_rate flat to slightly up, depth_nan_ratio and ring_count_error unchanged; if F1 drops instead, the template has overshot and round 2 (760) remains the best candidate.

- Observation: Round 2 (binary 135, oblique/horizontal Hough 38/34, maxLineGap 36, pattern_tolerance 10; SAM K_height 760, angle 6.12, width 1200) moved the proxy UP 0.671 -> 0.744 (anchor 0.681), crossing into the accept band, and the gain was carried entirely by correspondence_f1@15 0.447 -> 0.581 with sam_fill_rate essentially flat (0.649 -> 0.651), depth_nan_ratio 0.105, ring_count_error 0 and recentre residual 3.2 cm all unchanged. Stages 1-3 therefore remain healthy and the round-2 detected-line picture is exactly what we wanted: 10 magenta verticals == 10 rings, all 10 snap prompts at y~1550 (propagated = regime, det_fallback_ratio 1.0 expected under uniform_k_snap), a clean upper K-row oblique family at y~1445-1480 (green) and a lower K-row family at y~1620-1640 (red) present in 9-10 of 10 columns, plus short blue horizontals on the bolt-pocket rows at y~800 and y~2300 and no stray pocket-edge fragments. The mask panel still shows the K label as a band that starts on the upper joint and extends down into the bright bolt-pocket band (y~1600-1690), i.e. the K/B2 boundary is still biased below the detected ~1630 lower joint and the stacked B2/A3 boundary below the ~2300 pocket row; the lower K-row lines are the largest remaining set of joint lines whose explaining boundary is off by more than 15 px. Both between-round deltas are consistent with a single monotone mechanism: undersizing the K template (823.8 -> 760) lets the B2 template/prompts claim the pocket band and pulls the K/B2 and B2/A3 boundaries up toward their joint lines, while tighter Hough removed unexplained fragments (precision).

## 3-6 — round 3

- Observation: Round 2 (binary 135, oblique/horizontal Hough 38/34, maxLineGap 36, pattern_tolerance 10; SAM K_height 760, angle 6.12, width 1200) moved the proxy UP 0.671 -> 0.744 (anchor 0.681), crossing into the accept band, and the gain was carried entirely by correspondence_f1@15 0.447 -> 0.581 with sam_fill_rate essentially flat (0.649 -> 0.651), depth_nan_ratio 0.105, ring_count_error 0 and recentre residual 3.2 cm all unchanged. Stages 1-3 therefore remain healthy and the round-2 detected-line picture is exactly what we wanted: 10 magenta verticals == 10 rings, all 10 snap prompts at y~1550 (propagated = regime, det_fallback_ratio 1.0 expected under uniform_k_snap), a clean upper K-row oblique family at y~1445-1480 (green) and a lower K-row family at y~1620-1640 (red) present in 9-10 of 10 columns, plus short blue horizontals on the bolt-pocket rows at y~800 and y~2300 and no stray pocket-edge fragments. The mask panel still shows the K label as a band that starts on the upper joint and extends down into the bright bolt-pocket band (y~1600-1690), i.e. the K/B2 boundary is still biased below the detected ~1630 lower joint and the stacked B2/A3 boundary below the ~2300 pocket row; the lower K-row lines are the largest remaining set of joint lines whose explaining boundary is off by more than 15 px. Both between-round deltas are consistent with a single monotone mechanism: undersizing the K template (823.8 -> 760) lets the B2 template/prompts claim the pocket band and pulls the K/B2 and B2/A3 boundaries up toward their joint lines, while tighter Hough removed unexplained fragments (precision).
- Rule: experiences.md §7 tuning order: orientation, centreline (3.2 cm < 10 cm gate), band (retention 0.69, NaN 10.5%) and detection (line count == ring count, K-row pair present in every column) all validate, so only SAM geometry (step 5) is left to tune, and §6 says SAM block geometry is worth tuning exactly in this graded regime (all labels present, one boundary systematically biased). §9/§11: on continuous t3 use the proxy delta as the directional signal and corroborate against the artifacts; round 1 falsified r_relax_hough (F1 down) and round 2 confirmed the r_tighten_hough + template-undersize direction (F1 up 0.13, evidence features flat), so the last round should continue the confirmed gradient with a smaller step rather than open a new axis. Ontology d10/d12 -> f_template_mismatch / f_over_under_coverage -> r_fix_template / r_balance_coverage: when the K mask over-extends past the detected lower joint into a high-contrast pocket band, shrink the K block height (within family bounds) so the neighbouring B2 template competes for the band. §10 anti-pattern check: no orientation flips, no seed sweeps, no NaN chasing, no stage-1 rerun (stage1_unlocked=false).
- Failure mode: stage_5_segmentation.f_over_under_coverage / f_template_mismatch (residual): the K mask still bleeds downward into the bolt-pocket band below the detected lower K joint (~1630), keeping the K/B2 and, by stacking, the B2/A3 label boundaries >15 px from their joint lines; detection (stage 4) is now clean and is not the limiting stage.
- Rationale: Hold detection exactly at the round-2 values (binary_threshold 135, hough_threshold_oblique 38, hough_threshold_horizontal 34, maxLineGap_oblique 36, pattern_tolerance 10, uniform_k_snap true): they gave a complete 10/10 K-row pair with no spurious fragments, and raising the thresholds further would risk dropping the lower K-row segments that are already fragmented at x~1300-1400 and x~2100-2200 -- those are precisely the lines that the SAM move is meant to turn from unexplained into explained, so losing them would cap the achievable recall. Relaxing them again was falsified in round 1. On SAM, continue the confirmed direction with a smaller, bounded step: K_height 760 -> 720 mm (152 -> 144 px at ~0.2 px/mm), i.e. ~4 px shorter per side about the y~1550 snap row, so the K template now spans ~1478-1622 and the B2 template starts ~8 px higher inside the pocket band than in round 2; this should pull the K/B2 label boundary the remaining distance up onto the ~1630 joint line and shift the B2/A3 boundary toward the ~2300 pocket row, raising correspondence_f1@15 further. Stop at 720 rather than the 700 floor so the upper K boundary (which is already supported at ~1475 by the green family) is not dragged below its joint: a 4 px per-side shrink keeps it inside the 15 px tolerance even if SAM follows the template edge exactly. segment_width stays 1200 (vertical ring spacing ~240 px matches) and angle stays at the T3 taper prior 6.12 because taper only affects x-boundaries and there is no ring-boundary miss in the overlay. Unfolding is untouched (stage 1 locked, residual 3.2 cm); denoising and enhancing stay at anchor because retention, NaN and joint contrast are all healthy and none of the evidence features moved in rounds 1-2. Nudge enhancing.curvature_threshold 0.0005 -> 0.0004 so slightly fewer joint-edge points are treated as flat surface during densification, preserving joint discontinuities in the projected map; the change is small enough to keep depth_nan_ratio in the family-normal band. Vertical Hough, denoising band (tunnel priors, load-bearing for transfer) and SAM geometry (segment_width 1800 mm matches the measured 361 px pitch; K_height/angle at T5 priors) are intentionally unchanged so the proxy delta is attributable to detection. Expected signal: det_real_detection_ratio and oblique yield rise, prompt y-positions follow the K/AB pattern, correspondence_f1@15 increases with sam_fill_rate held near 0.76; a proxy rise driven only by evidence features with fill rate dropping would be treated as suspect.

(4) hough_threshold_horizontal 50 -> 45 and maxLineGap_horizontal 10 -> 15: keep horizontal fallback usable but not dominant. (5) pattern_tolerance -> 15 px: K/AB pixel pattern is 245/745 px at 5 mm/px; a moderate tolerance accepts real intersections that are a few px off the design pattern instead of discarding them into the 'assume' path. hough_threshold_vertical is left unchanged because 10 verticals already match 10 rings; denoising mask_r stays at T5 priors (retention and NaN are healthy; §4 warns against per-scan band tuning); sam.* stays at T5 priors (segment_width 1800, K_height 1226.97, angle 9.8). ## 4-1 — round 3

- Observation: Two detection-only rounds were proxy-neutral: anchor 0.5518 (correspondence_f1@15 0.225) -> round 1 0.5453 (0.213) -> round 2 0.5503 (0.222); sam_fill_rate is pinned at 0.7499, ring_count_error 0, depth_nan_ratio 0.2233, residual 2.4 cm, so stages 1-3 remain healthy and nothing outside detection has moved. The round-2 detected_lines map now shows ~20 horizontal seam traces and their spacing confirms the tunnel priors: within-ring seams sit ~745 px apart (AB = 3727 mm at 5 mm/px) and the K block spans ~250 px (K = 1227 mm), while the 10 verticals sit at a 360 px pitch (segment_width 1800 mm). SAM geometry priors are therefore correct and are NOT the source of the low correspondence. What the overlay and prompt map show instead is a split by prompt type: the five rings that received REAL prompts (positive_slope at x~1980, midpoints at x~2340/2700/3060, negative_slope at x~3420) carry the green (supported) boundaries, whereas the five rings still on 'assume' fallbacks (x~190, 550, 900, 1260, 1620; y alternating 1120/1550) are almost entirely red. The real K positions found in the right half (y ~ 860, 1350, 2620, 3830, 1100) do not follow a two-position alternation, so the assumed y values are wrong for the left-half rings and every template boundary in those rings is offset; sam_segment_size_cv 1.06 (vs ~0.3 implied by the K/AB template) is the per-block echo of that crop offset. Oblique yield is the bottleneck: only ~7 oblique segments were kept, several are short (e.g. the positive-slope trace at x 1380-1550, y~1290 inside ring 1620 stops before the ring-centre vertical, so it cannot produce an intersection), and the left half of the map, where joints are fainter and voids denser, produced none.
- Rule: Ontology d8 (m_spacing_regularity poor: assumed centres do not match the K/AB rotation actually observed) -> stage_4_detection.f_pattern_mismatch, propagating via inter_stage_dependencies to stage_5_segmentation.f_crop_offset (template boundaries land off-seam -> red boundaries, inflated sam_segment_size_cv). The orchestration hint says defer to the upstream stage before tuning SAM, and experiences s6 says SAM geometry is only worth tuning in the graded regime once priors are verified; here the priors are verified correct from the seam spacing, so SAM keys stay at prior. The upstream driver is d7 f_missing_lines on the OBLIQUE path specifically (K-edge traces too short to cross the ring-centre vertical), remediated by r_relax_hough applied to p_hough_oblique: lower threshold and raise maxLineGap to their bounds so broken K-edge traces are joined into segments long enough to intersect the vertical. Guard against f_spurious_lines: det_n_points is fixed at 10 (one centre per ring), the [5,10] deg angle filter and pattern_tolerance 20 px against K 245 / AB 745 px remain active, and the vertical count is pinned (10 == ring_count, experiences s5). Round-2 horizontal / binarisation settings are retained because they raised seam-line yield without adding spurious horizontals. Denoising and enhancing stay at tunnel priors (experiences s3/s4: 22% NaN is family-normal for 4-x, do not chase NaN, keep the r-band at priors); unfolding is locked (stage1_unlocked false).
- Failure mode: stage_4_detection.f_pattern_mismatch (5/10 rings on wrong 'assume' K positions -> stage_5 f_crop_offset), caused upstream by f_missing_lines on the oblique K-edge path
- Rationale: Detection overlay that converts 'assume' rings into real prompts. hough_threshold_oblique 35 -> 25 (bound) and maxLineGap_oblique 70 -> 80 (bound): the tapered K-block edges are ~360 px long at 5-10 deg but appear as dotted traces in the binarised outlier map; at the fixed minLineLength the lower accumulator threshold lets faint left-half traces register and the larger gap bridges the breaks so each K edge becomes ONE segment that reaches the ring-centre vertical, which is the geometric requirement for line_segment_vertical_intersection to fire. A K edge found on either side yields a positive_slope / negative_slope centre; both sides yield a midpoint, which is exactly the prompt type that produced supported boundaries in rings 2340/2700/3060. binary_threshold 100, hough_threshold_horizontal 30, maxLineGap_horizontal 25 and pattern_tolerance 20 are carried over from round 2 (the horizontal seam traces are what the SC overlay needs to explain label boundaries, and the 20 px tolerance is what accepts real intersections into the K/AB pattern). hough_threshold_vertical untouched: 10 verticals == 10 rings. SAM segment_width / K_height / angle stay at prior because the detected seam spacing (745 px AB, ~250 px K, 360 px ring pitch) confirms them; changing them would fit noise. Denoising mask_r and enhancing curvature_threshold stay at anchor so the proxy delta is attributable to prompt-centre quality. Expected GT-free signal: det_real_detection_ratio rises above 0.5 (fewer green 'assume' diamonds in initial_prompt_points, more stars / triangles in the left half), more green supported boundaries in the left-half rings of the SC overlay, correspondence_f1@15 up from 0.222, sam_segment_size_cv down, sam_fill_rate / ring_count_error / depth_nan_ratio unchanged. If det_real_detection_ratio does not rise, the left-half joints are below what stage-4 binarisation can recover and the remaining lever is the out-of-bounds enhancing depth_threshold (r_lower_depth_threshold), not further Hough or SAM tuning.

## 4-5 — round 3

- Observation: Round 2 (a second uniform relaxation of every detection knob: binary 110->104, oblique 35/65 -> 28/75, horizontal 40/20 -> 32/26, tolerance 15->18) REVERSED the round-1 gain: proxy_scaled 0.656 -> 0.625, correspondence_f1@15 0.419 -> 0.363. Every other lean feature is bit-identical across anchor/R1/R2 (sam_fill_rate 0.7353592515275288, depth_nan_ratio 0.2155, ring_count_error 0, residual 3.4 cm), so the proxy on this scan is a pure function of F1 and the stage-5 label map is pinned to the geometric fallback (10 verticals + K-row gate); det_real_detection_ratio is still 0.6 (initial_prompt_points.png: 4 'assume' of 10). A parabola through the three points along the shared 'relax everything' ray (anchor t=0 0.609, R1 t=1 0.656, R2 t=2 0.625) peaks at t~1.1, i.e. no further gain is available along that ray. The R2 detected_lines.png explains why: the label boundaries in the correspondence overlay are almost all HORIZONTAL block seams confined to one ring column (~370 px = 1.8 m segment width), and in the anchor overlay horizontals are predominantly cyan (explained) while obliques are predominantly orange (unexplained). R2 added ~12 oblique segments of which several are 350-550 px long and cross one or more magenta ring verticals (e.g. ~x 150-700 @ y 3450, x 1800-2200 @ y 2700, x 2550-2900 @ y 4200): a real tapered block edge cannot span two rings, so these are spurious lines bridged across the bolt-pocket 'V' texture by maxLineGap_oblique 75 with a near-floor accumulator of 28. Those lines lower precision without supporting any label boundary. The ~25 horizontals in R2, by contrast, sit on faint dotted seam rows and are the precision-safe recall source that most plausibly produced the R1 gain.
- Rule: d9 / f_spurious_lines on the OBLIQUE family (cross-ring oblique segments, unexplained in the overlay) -> r_tighten_hough: raise hough_threshold_oblique and lower maxLineGap_oblique so a line can no longer accumulate votes across a ring joint. Simultaneously d7 / f_missing_lines still holds on the HORIZONTAL family (most horizontal label boundaries remain unsupported) -> keep r_relax_hough for horizontals at the R2 setting. The two Hough families share no accumulator, so decoupling them is a valid single coordinated overlay; the round-2 stop rule ('if F1 falls, revert toward round-1 values') is honoured by reverting binary_threshold and pattern_tolerance to R1 and moving oblique back past R1 toward the anchor. Per experiences s5 leave hough_threshold_vertical alone (10 verticals == 10 rings); per s4/s7 leave denoising, enhancing and unfolding (locked) untouched; per s6 defer SAM geometry because the label boundaries in the overlay already coincide with the visible seams in the depth map, so there is no GT-blind evidence of a template offset to fix.
- Failure mode: stage_4_detection.f_spurious_lines (oblique family: long cross-ring segments admitted by low accumulator + large gap, unexplained by any boundary -> precision drop that erased the R1 recall gain) with residual stage_4_detection.f_missing_lines on the horizontal family (block-seam boundaries still recall-limited).
- Rationale: Round 3 is a decoupled, partial-factor step designed to beat R1 rather than to bracket it: (1) hough_threshold_oblique 28 -> 45 and maxLineGap_oblique 75 -> 40 tighten the oblique family beyond R1 (35/65) and back toward the T4 prior (50/50), so oblique votes must come from a continuous ~100 px tapered-edge run inside one ring; the cross-ring spurious segments of R2 should disappear, raising precision, while genuine K-block tapered edges (within a 370 px column, short gaps) are still admitted. (2) hough_threshold_horizontal 32 and maxLineGap_horizontal 26 stay at the R2 values: a within-ring seam is <= 370 px with minLineLength 105 fixed, so gap 26 cannot bridge across ring joints, and these knobs are the plausible source of the R1 recall gain. (3) binary_threshold reverts 104 -> 110 (R1, the best-known binarisation; 104 admits more V-texture noise into both accumulators). (4) pattern_tolerance reverts 18 -> 15 (R1); it only affects prompt acceptance and cannot move F1 while SAM is on fallback, so R1's value is the safe choice. hough_threshold_vertical, denoising, enhancing and SAM are untouched so any proxy move is attributable to the oblique/horizontal decoupling. Expected: fewer, shorter oblique lines (orange count down), horizontals unchanged or slightly up -> correspondence_f1@15 above the R1 0.419 and proxy_scaled above 0.656 (target >= 0.70); ring_count_error stays 0, depth_nan_ratio and sam_fill_rate remain identical. If instead F1 lands between R2 and R1, the R1 gain was oblique-driven and R1 remains the selection.

Together with round 1 (oblique Hough 50->35 / gap 70 / binarisation 110: prompt set unchanged, only a NaN wobble from the curvature nudge) this proves the oblique-pair / pattern path is non-identifiable here: two rounds of detecting overlays on that path moved the proxy by <0.001. Stages 1-3 remain healthy and untouched (residual 1.9 cm, orient_axis_corr 0.91, NaN 0.193 in the 19-22% complex-family band, retention 0.77, 10 verticals for 10 rings). Re-reading detected_lines.png: the circumferential joints (blue, horizontal HoughLinesP) are the only lines that enter the SC joint-line set, and they are sparse — roughly 25 blue segments for 70 block boundaries, each a short 105-180 px piece of a dotted joint trace, and several rings (x=180, 540, 900) have almost none. In the SC overlay the right panel is dominated by orange (unexplained) lines while the left panel shows long runs of red (unsupported) boundaries in rings whose K centre is correct to within 6 px (1260, 1980, 2700, 3060, 3420), i.e. boundaries are unsupported because no line was detected at the joint, not because the crop is offset. Separately, round 2's pixel-precise measurement of the four clean K pairs gave 252-255 px against the 245.4 px prior (K_height 1226.97 mm -> measured ~1265 mm), a consistent +3% under-height of the K template.
- Rule: Ontology d7 (m_line_yield low, horizontal fallback / sparse horizontal joint set) -> stage_4_detection.f_missing_lines on the HORIZONTAL path, with the r_relax_hough precondition satisfied (no f_depth_holes: NaN in family band; f_joint_oversmoothed cannot be addressed in-bounds and the oblique path is proven inert). Remediation r_relax_hough applied to p_hough_horizontal (lower threshold, raise maxLineGap toward the 5-1 recovery values 35/80 cited in experiences §5), which is the only detection knob that directly enlarges the SC joint-line set feeding correspondence_f1@15. Secondary: r_fix_template (stage_5 f_template_mismatch, graded per-class regime per experiences §6 — all 7 labels present, none collapsed) using the measured K pair separation to set p_block_K_height; precondition satisfied since resolution is unchanged and stages 1-4 are validated (§7 tuning order reaches SAM geometry only now that detection knobs are shown null). Experiences §10: do not re-tune knobs shown null in previous rounds (oblique Hough, binarisation, pattern_tolerance, curvature are all left at anchor).
- Failure mode: f_missing_lines on the horizontal joint path (sparse circumferential joint lines -> block boundaries unsupported and joints unexplained in the SC correspondence even where prompts are correct), plus a mild f_template_mismatch (K template ~8 px shorter than the measured K block). The oblique/pattern path (f_pattern_mismatch) tested in rounds 1-2 is confirmed non-identifiable and is dropped.
- Rationale: Switch to the horizontal joint path as planned in round 2. detecting.hough_threshold_horizontal 50 -> 40 and detecting.maxLineGap_horizontal 10 -> 20: the joints in the outlier map are dotted traces with gaps of 10-20 px between bolt-pocket-free segments, so doubling the gap lets the existing 105 px minLineLength be met by traces that currently break into sub-threshold pieces, and a moderate threshold drop (40, not 25-35) adds votes for faint joints without letting the rows of bolt-pocket marks (isolated 10-20 px blobs on ~250 px spacing) accumulate into spurious horizontals. Expected: more blue lines at true circumferential joints in rings 180/540/900 and the second half-map of every ring, raising the supported-boundary count in the five already-aligned rings and the explained-line count, so correspondence_f1@15 rises from 0.316 while sam_fill_rate stays ~0.756 and ring_count stays 10 (verticals untouched at prior). Because the horizontal set was never a prompt source here (all 8 real prompts come from oblique pairs), the prompt centres should be unchanged and any proxy delta is attributable to the joint-line set plus the template edit. sam.K_height 1226.97 -> 1265.0 mm (253 px) aligns the K template with the measured 252-255 px K pairs; this moves the K edges and the adjoining B1/B2 outer edges by ~4 px each, small enough not to disturb fill rate but enough to bring boundaries sitting at 12-18 px off a joint inside the 15 px tolerance. segment_width (1800 mm = measured 360 px pitch) and angle (9.8 deg; measured 9.0-9.2 deg differs by <5 px across a ring) stay at priors; denoising band stays at tunnel priors (load-bearing for transfer). full=false; unfolding omitted (stage1_unlocked is false). Suspect-signal check: if the proxy rises while sam_fill_rate drops or the joint-line count explodes with precision falling (mostly orange lines inside blocks), the horizontal relaxation is over-shooting and the anchor should be preferred at selection.

## 5-3 — round 3

- Observation: Round-2 artifacts (detected_lines / initial_prompt_points, proxy 0.686, best so far): 10 synthetic ring-centre verticals at x = 180 + 360.7*i (pitch 360.7 px = 1803 mm, so segment_width 1800 is already right). Seven rings carry real K prompts; rings 0, 1, 9 are still 'assume' (det_fallback_ratio 0.30 unchanged since anchor). Pixel-measuring the round-2 oblique pairs at the ring-centre x gives K top/bottom edges of 1280/1531 (ring 4), 2518/2769 (ring 5), 3259/3511 (ring 3), 4011/4264 (ring 7), 3514/3768 (ring 8): K height 250.6-253.6 px (mean 251.8 px = 1259 mm), slopes |8.1-10.0| deg (mean ~8.9), so sam.K_height 1250 / angle 9.0 are converged. The edge-ring traces that do exist (green x 221-367 at y~4560 in ring 0; red dashes x 3253-3345 at y~805 in ring 9) stop short of the ring-centre vertical, and the plotted outlier map has ~15-70 joint pixels in rings 0/1/9 vs ~1000 in ring 4: there is no pixel evidence for Hough to extend, so oblique relaxation is exhausted (28/80/100 is already at the evidence-safe edge). Horizontal joints are a different story: the blue horizontals recovered at 45/15 sit exactly on the K lattice (ring 4: 535 = 1280-745, 3012 ~ 1531+2*745, 3758 ~ 1531+3*745; ring 5: 1035 = 2518-2*745, 1780 = 2518-745, 3510 = 2769+745), i.e. every detected horizontal in a real-prompt ring is a true A/B joint that supports a label boundary, but each central ring only has ~5 of its 7 lattice rows covered (e.g. ring 4 rows 2276 and 4511, ring 5 rows 283 and 4259 have no line although dotted joint traces are visible there). The unchanged sam_fill_rate (0.7368 across anchor/R1/R2 despite K_height/angle changes) shows fill is not reachable with the bounded knobs; correspondence_f1@15 is the only lever that has moved the proxy (+0.011 f1 -> +0.007 proxy in R2).
- Rule: sam4tun_ontology stage_4_detection d7/f_missing_lines -> r_relax_hough ('lower Hough threshold / raise maxLineGap toward tunnel prior; precondition: rule out upstream f_joint_oversmoothed / f_depth_holes' - depth_nan 0.20 and retention 0.75 are family-normal per experiences s3, so upstream is healthy). experiences s5: line count must stay consistent with ring count (verticals are synthetic, 10 = 10, untouched) and s7 tuning order: stages 1-3 healthy, detection before SAM; s9: use the proxy as a directional signal and corroborate with artifacts (measured lattice agreement above). experiences s4: keep denoising mask_r at tunnel priors. T3 prior in tunnel_priors uses horizontal Hough threshold 30 with minLineLength 50, so a horizontal threshold of ~30 is a known-good operating point for dotted joint traces; the fixed T4/T5 minLineLength 105 px remains as the spurious-line guard.
- Failure mode: stage_4_detection.f_missing_lines (horizontal joint traces under-detected: ~2 of 7 lattice rows per real-prompt ring have no supporting line, so correct label boundaries are counted as unsupported in correspondence_f1@15); residual stage_4 f_missing_lines on rings 0/1/9 is evidence-limited (K-in-void), not parameter-limited, and is deliberately not chased further.
- Rationale: Final round: keep the round-2 configuration (best proxy 0.686; binary 100, oblique 28/80, pattern_tolerance 20, curvature 0.0004, K_height 1250, angle 9.0) and change ONLY the horizontal Hough pair, so the attribution is clean: detecting.hough_threshold_horizontal 45 -> 32 and detecting.maxLineGap_horizontal 15 -> 25. With 3x3 dilation each joint dot contributes ~9 votes, so 32 votes needs ~4 collinear dots within the fixed 105 px minLineLength, and a 25 px gap bridges the dot spacing seen along the dotted A/B joint rows that currently carry no blue line; 32 stays above the T3 operating point (30) and well above the bound floor (25). Expected GT-blind signal: more horizontal lines that land on the K lattice of the seven real-prompt rings, converting red (unsupported) label boundaries to green and lifting correspondence_f1@15 by roughly +0.02-0.04 (each extra supported row in each central ring is ~1.4% of the 70 boundaries), which is the +0.014 proxy needed to cross the 0.70 accept band; precision of the new lines should be high because rounds 1-2 showed that every horizontal found in a real-prompt ring lies on the measured lattice. Horizontal lines do not drive prompt centres here (ring 1 already had a horizontal crossing its vertical and still fell back to 'assume'), so prompt geometry, ring count (10/10) and fill rate are expected to be unchanged; the downside is bounded to a small precision loss if a few noise lines appear in the joint-poor edge rings. Oblique Hough, binary threshold and pattern_tolerance are frozen at round-2 values because the edge-ring traces lack pixel support to reach the ring-centre verticals (round-2 lesson: treat rings 0/1/9 as K-in-void and stop tuning oblique Hough). sam.K_height 1250 and angle 9.0 are retained as measured (251.8 px, ~8.9 deg); segment_width stays 1800 (measured 1803 mm). Denoising left at T5 priors; unfolding omitted (stage1_unlocked false, residual 3.5 cm).

## 5-4 — round 3

- Observation: Round 2 (oblique Hough at the bound edges: binary 100, threshold 25, maxLineGap 80, tolerance 20) falsified the 'more oblique lines' route: proxy fell 0.6924 -> 0.6726 and correspondence_f1@15 fell 0.4676 -> 0.4312 while fill rate, NaN ratio, ring_count_error and recentre residual (2.1 cm) stayed bit-identical, so the whole loss is prompt placement. detected_lines.png at round 2 shows ~12 short oblique segments (8 positive, 4 negative), almost all confined to the dense middle rings x 1100-2500, and initial_prompt_points.png is WORSE in kind: 3 assume (x~180, 540, 3430), 5 midpoint (x~900, 1260, 1620, 1990, 2350) and only 2 positive_slope (x~2720, 3070); the negative_slope prompt and one positive_slope prompt of round 1 degraded to midpoint because extra oblique candidates produced intersections that fail check_distance_pattern. det_real_detection_ratio is unchanged at 0.7 (the three edge-ring 'assume' fallbacks persist). The untried evidence is the HORIZONTAL joint family: in depth_map_viridis the circumferential A/B-block joints are visible as continuous faint lines across all ten rings, including the sparse outer rings (x<900 and x>2500), and in the outlier map they appear as dotted runs at y~520, 1270, 2020, 2760, 4000 in the middle columns but as sparse, broken dots in the outer columns. At the anchor horizontal Hough (threshold 50, maxLineGap 10, minLineLength 105) these outer-ring dotted joints do not link: the blue horizontal lines in detected_lines.png are concentrated in x 1100-2500 with almost none in x<900 (only y~2020 and y~2760 near x 900-1100) and few in x>2900. Pattern matching (check_distance_pattern over intersection y's spaced by K_height_pixel 245 / AB_height_pixel 745) therefore has no intersections at all in the edge rings (-> assume) and too few horizontal anchors in the midpoint rings. Stages 1-3 remain healthy (orient_invariant_ok=1, |corr| 0.77, residual 2.1 cm, NaN 0.198 family-normal, retention 0.77, 10 verticals for 10 rings), so no upstream move is warranted and unfolding is locked.
- Rule: d7 (m_line_yield low AND horizontal fallback triggered -> f_missing_lines) still fires, but rounds 1-2 show the oblique branch of r_relax_hough is exhausted in this parameter box (yield rose only in already-detected rings and over-relaxation created f_pattern_mismatch via spurious oblique intersections). The remaining in-bounds branch of r_relax_hough is the horizontal HoughLinesP tuple (p_hough_horizontal, fallback_dependent): lower hough_threshold_horizontal and raise maxLineGap_horizontal so dotted circumferential joints in the outer rings link into segments that intersect the ring verticals and feed check_distance_pattern with AB-spaced anchors. Precondition satisfied: f_joint_oversmoothed at the upstream stage cannot be treated in-bounds (depth_threshold_low/high not admissible) and f_depth_holes is ruled out (NaN family-normal, voids are structural). Experiences s5: the sibling T5 case 5-1 recovered detection by lowering both oblique AND horizontal thresholds with ring-consistent verticals; s7: verticals already equal ring count so hough_threshold_vertical stays; s9: proxy must move through the coherence feature correspondence_f1@15, not evidence features, so denoising/enhancing stay frozen. Round-2 lesson applied: do NOT go to the bound edges (threshold 25 / gap 30); choose a moderate dose so minLineLength 105 (unchanged, out of bounds) keeps screening the ~30 px bolt-pocket chevrons. Oblique knobs and pattern_tolerance are returned to anchor values (omitted) so the round is attributable to the horizontal branch alone. SAM geometry (s6/s7 step 5) is again deferred: 6/10 K centres are still guesses and K_height/angle cannot correct a mis-placed prompt; segment_width is already consistent with the observed 360 px ring pitch (1800 mm).
- Failure mode: stage_4_detection.f_missing_lines on the horizontal-joint branch (outer-ring circumferential joints do not survive Hough at threshold 50 / maxLineGap 10 -> no intersections in the edge rings -> 'assume' fallback on 3/10 rings and under-constrained pattern matching -> 'midpoint' on 5/10 rings), with upstream stage_3_upsample.f_joint_oversmoothed (not treatable within bounds), cascading to stage_4.f_pattern_mismatch and stage_5_segmentation.f_crop_offset (guessed K centres -> shifted block boundaries -> unsupported label boundaries, correspondence_f1@15 0.43-0.47).
- Rationale: The anchor proxy 0.692 sits 0.008 under the accept band and the only lean feature far from healthy is correspondence_f1@15 (0.467), which is set by prompt-centre y placement. Two rounds established that the oblique-Hough axis is flat-to-negative in this box, so round 3 spends the final budget on the one detection axis with fresh evidence and a clear mechanism: horizontal joints are the most numerous, longest, and most regularly spaced (AB_height_pixel 745) joint lines on this map, they are visibly present in the outer rings on the depth map, and they feed the same intersection + distance-pattern step that decides whether a ring gets a real K centre or a guess. Move hough_threshold_horizontal 50 -> 30 (accumulator votes; dotted outer-ring joints have roughly 30-60 lit pixels per 105 px window after 3x3 dilation) and maxLineGap_horizontal 10 -> 25 (bridges the 10-25 px gaps between dots without joining separate joints, which are >=245 px apart). Both are interior points of the bounds ([25,70], [5,30]) chosen deliberately after round 2 showed bound-edge stampedes generate spurious candidates. minLineLength 105 stays (out of bounds) so the periodic bolt-pocket chevrons (~30 px) and isolated speckle cannot become lines; the [5,10]-degree angle filter, ring-vertical intersection, merge_close_points(6) and check_distance_pattern still gate every candidate. Oblique keys, binary_threshold and pattern_tolerance are omitted (back to anchor) and denoising/enhancing/sam are untouched, so depth_nan_ratio, sam_fill_rate and ring_count_error must remain identical and any proxy change is attributable to correspondence_f1@15 via horizontal-line-driven prompt placement. Expected signature: more blue horizontal segments in x<900 and x>2900, edge-ring 'assume' prompts converted to pattern-derived prompts, midpoint prompts snapping onto the AB lattice, det_real_detection_ratio 0.7 -> >=0.8, sam_segment_size_cv down from 1.11, correspondence_f1@15 up toward >=0.5 (proxy >=0.70 crosses the accept band). Failure signature: if horizontal yield rises but prompt types are unchanged, detection for 5-4 is non-identifiable across all in-bounds Hough knobs and the selector should keep the anchor / round 1; the true remediation is stage-3 joint enhancement (r_lower_depth_threshold), which is outside this campaign's bounds.
